- **agent-architecture-critic**: Adversarial opponent of the architect. Runs INSIDE the architecture phase, after agent-architect produces a plan, as the AUTONOMOUS GATE before implementation (replaces human plan review). Attacks the plan — the unlisted writer, the unconsidered retry path, the silent assumption, the DB change that may not be needed, the unjustified new entity/endpoint/dependency.
